+1|// Physics engine with fixed timestep and swept collision detection
+2|
+3|const PHYSICS_STEP = 1 / 120; // Fixed timestep in seconds
+4|const BALL_RADIUS = 6;
+5|const MIN_BALL_SPEED = 100;
+6|const PADDLE_HEIGHT = 15;
+7|const PADDLE_BOTTOM = 720;
+8|const GAME_WIDTH = 800;
+9|const GAME_HEIGHT = 800;
+10|
+11|// Swept circle collision detection
+12|function sweptCircleVsRect(ball, rect, dt) {
+13|  const { x: bx, y: by, vx, vy } = ball;
+14|  const { x: rx, y: ry, w, h } = rect;
+15|  
+16|  // Project ball position at next frame
+17|  const nextX = bx + vx * dt;
+18|  const nextY = by + vy * dt;
+19|  
+20|  // Find closest point on rect to ball's swept path
+21|  const closestX = Math.max(rx, Math.min(nextX, rx + w));
+22|  const closestY = Math.max(ry, Math.min(nextY, ry + h));
+23|  
+24|  // Check if swept circle intersects
+25|  const dx = bx - closestX;
+26|  const dy = by - closestY;
+27|  const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
+28|  
+29|  if (distSq < BALL_RADIUS * BALL_RADIUS) {
+30|    // Collision detected
+31|    // Find exact collision point by binary search
+32|    let t = 0, dt_test = dt;
+33|    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+34|      const testX = bx + vx * t;
+35|      const testY = by + vy * t;
+36|      const cx = Math.max(rx, Math.min(testX, rx + w));
+37|      const cy = Math.max(ry, Math.min(testY, ry + h));
+38|      const dx2 = testX - cx;
+39|      const dy2 = testY - cy;
+40|      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx2 * dx2 + dy2 * dy2);
+41|      
+42|      if (dist > BALL_RADIUS) {
+43|        t += dt_test;
+44|      } else {
+45|        t -= dt_test;
+46|      }
+47|      dt_test *= 0.5;
+48|    }
+49|    
+50|    // Normal to collision face
+51|    let nx = 0, ny = 0;
+52|    const collideX = bx + vx * t;
+53|    const collideY = by + vy * t;
+54|    
+55|    const distLeft = Math.abs(collideX - rx);
+56|    const distRight = Math.abs(collideX - (rx + w));
+57|    const distTop = Math.abs(collideY - ry);
+58|    const distBottom = Math.abs(collideY - (ry + h));
+59|    
+60|    const minDist = Math.min(distLeft, distRight, distTop, distBottom);
+61|    
+62|    if (minDist === distLeft) {
+63|      nx = -1;
+64|    } else if (minDist === distRight) {
+65|      nx = 1;
+66|    } else if (minDist === distTop) {
+67|      ny = -1;
+68|    } else {
+69|      ny = 1;
+70|    }
+71|    
+72|    return {
+73|      collision: true,
+74|      t: Math.min(t, dt),
+75|      nx, ny,
+76|      x: collideX,
+77|      y: collideY
+78|    };
+79|  }
+80|  
+81|  return { collision: false };
+82|}
+83|
+84|// Reflect velocity off surface
+85|function reflectVelocity(vx, vy, nx, ny) {
+86|  const dot = vx * nx + vy * ny;
+87|  return {
+88|    vx: vx - 2 * dot * nx,
+89|    vy: vy - 2 * dot * ny
+90|  };
+91|}
+92|
+93|// Enforce minimum speed
+94|function enforceMinimumSpeed(vx, vy, minSpeed) {
+95|  const speed = Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy);
+96|  if (speed < minSpeed && speed > 0) {
+97|    const factor = minSpeed / speed;
+98|    return { vx: vx * factor, vy: vy * factor };
+99|  }
+100|  return { vx, vy };
+101|}
+102|
+103|// Cap maximum speed
+104|function capSpeed(vx, vy, cap) {
+105|  const speed = Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy);
+106|  if (speed > cap) {
+107|    const factor = cap / speed;
+108|    return { vx: vx * factor, vy: vy * factor };
+109|  }
+110|  return { vx, vy };
+111|}
+112|
+113|// Paddle steering based on impact position
+114|function steerFromPaddle(ballX, paddleX, paddleWidth, baseVx, baseVy, 
recentMotion) {
+115|  const paddleCenter = paddleX + paddleWidth / 2;
+116|  const relPos = (ballX - paddleCenter) / (paddleWidth / 2);
+117|  
+118|  // Steer angle based on impact position
+119|  const maxAngle = Math.PI / 6; // 30 degrees
+120|  const angle = relPos * maxAngle;
+121|  const speed = Math.sqrt(baseVx * baseVx + baseVy * baseVy);
+122|  
+123|  let vx = speed * Math.sin(angle);
+124|  let vy = -Math.abs(baseVy); // Ensure upward
+125|  
+126|  // Add paddle motion influence
+127|  vx += recentMotion * 0.3;
+128|  
+129|  return { vx, vy };
+130|}
+131|
+132|// Simulate one fixed physics step
+133|function step(state, paddle, dt = PHYSICS_STEP) {
+134|  const { balls, bricks, drops, combo, power, powerSeconds } = state;
+135|  let { score, lives } = state;
+136|  let newCombo = combo;
+137|  let bricksDestroyed = 0;
+138|  let powerChanged = false;
+139|  let newPower = power;
+140|  let newPowerSeconds = powerSeconds;
+141|  const events = [];
+142|  
+143|  // Advance ball positions with collision detection
+144|  const newBalls = [];
+145|  let stepsRemaining = dt;
+146|  let substep = 0;
+147|  const maxSubsteps = 5;
+148|  
+149|  for (const ball of balls) {
+150|    if (!ball || ball.lost) {
+151|      if (ball) newBalls.push(ball);
+152|      continue;
+153|    }
+154|    
+155|    let currentBall = { ...ball };
+156|    let timeLeft = dt;
+157|    substep = 0;
+158|    
+159|    while (timeLeft > 1e-6 && substep < maxSubsteps) {
+160|      substep++;
+161|      const moveTime = Math.min(timeLeft, dt);
+162|      
+163|      // Check collisions with bricks
+164|      let collided = false;
+165|      for (let i = 0; i < bricks.length; i++) {
+166|        const brick = bricks[i];
+167|        if (brick.destroyed) continue;
+168|        
+169|        const brickRect = {
+170|          x: brick.x,
+171|          y: brick.y,
+172|          w: brick.w,
+173|          h: brick.h
+174|        };
+175|        
+176|        const collision = sweptCircleVsRect(currentBall, brickRect, 
moveTime);
+177|        
+178|        if (collision.collision) {
+179|          // Move ball to collision point
+180|          currentBall.x += currentBall.vx * collision.t;
+181|          currentBall.y += currentBall.vy * collision.t;
+182|          timeLeft -= collision.t;
+183|          
+184|          // Reflect velocity
+185|          let reflected = reflectVelocity(currentBall.vx, currentBall.vy, 
collision.nx, collision.ny);
+186|          
+187|          // Damage brick if not solid
+188|          if (brick.type !== 'solid') {
+189|            if (brick.type === 'normal') {
+190|              bricks[i].destroyed = true;
+191|              score += 100 * newCombo;
+192|              events.push({ type: 'brick', score: 100 * newCombo, combo: 
newCombo });
+193|              bricksDestroyed++;
+194|              
+195|              // Check for power-up
+196|              if (brick.drop) {
+197|                drops.push({
+198|                  type: brick.drop,
+199|                  x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,
+200|                  y: brick.y + brick.h / 2,
+201|                  vy: 100
+202|                });
+203|              }
+204|              
+205|              // Increase combo
+206|              if (newCombo < 5) newCombo++;
+207|            } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
+208|              if (!brick.damaged) {
+209|                bricks[i].damaged = true;
+210|                score += 75 * newCombo;
+211|                events.push({ type: 'brick', score: 75 * newCombo, combo: 
newCombo });
+212|                if (newCombo < 5) newCombo++;
+213|              } else {
+214|                bricks[i].destroyed = true;
+215|                score += 250 * newCombo;
+216|                events.push({ type: 'brick', score: 250 * newCombo, combo: 
newCombo });
+217|                bricksDestroyed++;
+218|                if (brick.drop) {
+219|                  drops.push({
+220|                    type: brick.drop,
+221|                    x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,
+222|                    y: brick.y + brick.h / 2,
+223|                    vy: 100
+224|                  });
+225|                }
+226|                if (newCombo < 5) newCombo++;
+227|              }
+228|            }
+229|          } else {
+230|            events.push({ type: 'solid-deflection' });
+231|          }
+232|          
+233|          currentBall.vx = reflected.vx;
+234|          currentBall.vy = reflected.vy;
+235|          collided = true;
+236|          break;
+237|        }
+238|      }
+239|      
+240|      if (!collided) {
+241|        // Check paddle collision
+242|        const paddleRect = {
+243|          x: paddle.x,
+244|          y: paddle.y - PADDLE_HEIGHT,
+245|          w: paddle.width,
+246|          h: PADDLE_HEIGHT
+247|        };
+248|        
+249|        const paddleCollision = sweptCircleVsRect(currentBall, paddleRect, 
timeLeft);
+250|        
+251|        if (paddleCollision.collision) {
+252|          currentBall.x += currentBall.vx * paddleCollision.t;
+253|          currentBall.y += currentBall.vy * paddleCollision.t;
+254|          timeLeft -= paddleCollision.t;
+255|          
+256|          // Steer from paddle impact
+257|          const steered = steerFromPaddle(
+258|            currentBall.x,
+259|            paddle.x,
+260|            paddle.width,
+261|            currentBall.vx,
+262|            currentBall.vy,
+263|            paddle.recentMotion || 0
+264|          );
+265|          
+266|          currentBall.vx = steered.vx;
+267|          currentBall.vy = steered.vy;
+268|          
+269|          // Reset combo on paddle hit
+270|          newCombo = 1;
+271|          currentBall.stuck = false;
+272|          
+273|          events.push({ type: 'paddle-contact', combo: newCombo });
+274|          collided = true;
+275|        } else {
+276|          // No collision - move freely
+277|          currentBall.x += currentBall.vx * timeLeft;
+278|          currentBall.y += currentBall.vy * timeLeft;
+279|          timeLeft = 0;
+280|        }
+281|      } else {
+282|        continue;
+283|      }
+284|    }
+285|    
+286|    // Apply speed constraints
+287|    let speedCapped = capSpeed(currentBall.vx, currentBall.vy, 520); // 
Default cap, should be level-specific
+288|    currentBall.vx = speedCapped.vx;
+289|    currentBall.vy = speedCapped.vy;
+290|    
+291|    let minSpeedEnforced = enforceMinimumSpeed(currentBall.vx, 
currentBall.vy, MIN_BALL_SPEED);
+292|    currentBall.vx = minSpeedEnforced.vx;
+293|    currentBall.vy = minSpeedEnforced.vy;
+294|    
+295|    // Check if ball is lost
+296|    if (currentBall.y > GAME_HEIGHT) {
+297|      currentBall.lost = true;
+298|      events.push({ type: 'ball-lost' });
+299|    }
+300|    
+301|    newBalls.push(currentBall);
+302|  }
+303|  
+304|  // Update power-up timer
+305|  if (newPower && newPowerSeconds > 0) {
+306|    newPowerSeconds -= dt;
+307|    if (newPowerSeconds <= 0) {
+308|      newPowerSeconds = 0;
+309|      newPower = null;
+310|      powerChanged = true;
+311|      events.push({ type: 'power-expired' });
+312|    }
+313|  }
+314|  
+315|  // Process drops
+316|  const newDrops = [];
+317|  for (const drop of drops) {
+318|    let dropY = drop.y + drop.vy * dt;
+319|    
+320|    // Check paddle collision
+321|    if (dropY + 8 > paddle.y - PADDLE_HEIGHT &&
+322|        dropY - 8 < paddle.y &&
+323|        drop.x > paddle.x &&
+324|        drop.x < paddle.x + paddle.width) {
+325|      
+326|      // Apply power-up
+327|      if (drop.type === 'wide') {
+328|        paddle.width = Math.min(177, paddle.width * 1.5);
+329|      } else if (drop.type === 'slow') {
+330|        for (let ball of newBalls) {
+331|          ball.vx *= 0.7;
+332|          ball.vy *= 0.7;
+333|        }
+334|      } else if (drop.type === 'multiball' && newBalls.length < 2) {
+335|        // Create secondary ball
+336|        const mainBall = newBalls.find(b => !b.lost);
+337|        if (mainBall) {
+338|          newBalls.push({
+339|            x: mainBall.x - 20,
+340|            y: mainBall.y - 20,
+341|            vx: mainBall.vx * 0.8,
+342|            vy: mainBall.vy * 0.8,
+343|            lost: false,
+344|            stuck: false
+345|          });
+346|        }
+347|      } else if (drop.type === 'sticky') {
+348|        const mainBall = newBalls.find(b => !b.lost);
+349|        if (mainBall) {
+350|          mainBall.stuck = true;
+351|        }
+352|      }
+353|      
+354|      // Reset or stack power-up
+355|      if (newPower === drop.type) {
+356|        newPowerSeconds = 20; // Reset timer, don't stack
+357|      } else if (!newPower) {
+358|        newPower = drop.type;
+359|        newPowerSeconds = 20;
+360|        powerChanged = true;
+361|      }
+362|      
+363|      events.push({ type: 'power-collected', power: drop.type });
+364|    } else if (dropY < GAME_HEIGHT) {
+365|      newDrops.push({ ...drop, y: dropY });
+366|    }
+367|  }
+368|  
+369|  return {
+370|    balls: newBalls,
+371|    score,
+372|    lives,
+373|    combo: newCombo,
+374|    bricks,
+375|    drops: newDrops,
+376|    power: newPower,
+377|    powerSeconds: newPowerSeconds,
+378|    events
+379|  };
+380|}
+381|
+382|module.exports = {
+383|  step,
+384|  PHYSICS_STEP,
+385|  BALL_RADIUS,
+386|  MIN_BALL_SPEED,
+387|  PADDLE_HEIGHT,
+388|  PADDLE_BOTTOM,
+389|  GAME_WIDTH,
+390|  GAME_HEIGHT
+391|};

handleSignin);\n  \n  document.querySelectorAll('.demo-btn').forEach(btn => {\n

email = btn.getAttribute('data-email');\n      
document.getElementById('email').value = email;\n      
document.getElementById('password').value = 'password123';\n      
document.getElementById('signin-form').dispatchEvent(new Event('submit'));\n    
});\n  });\n  \n  // Sign-out\n  
document.getElementById('signout-btn').addEventListener('click', 
handleSignout);\n  \n  // Game controls\n  
document.getElementById('launch-btn').addEventListener('click', launchBall);\n  
document.getElementById('pause-btn').addEventListener('click', togglePause);\n  
document.getElementById('restart-btn').addEventListener('click', restartRun);\n 
\n  // Tab navigation\n  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => 
{\n    btn.addEventListener('click', switchTab);\n  });\n  \n  // Canvas\n  
const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');\n  
canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', handleCanvasMouseMove);\n  
canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', handleCanvasTouchMove);\n  \n  // 
Keyboard\n  document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  
document.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n  \n  // Assist paddle\n  
document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').addEventListener('change', 
updateAssistPaddle);\n}\n\n// ============ AUTHENTICATION ============\n\nasync 
function handleSignin(e) {\n  e.preventDefault();\n  const email = 
document.getElementById('email').value;\n  const password = 
document.getElementById('password').value;\n  const errorDiv = 
document.getElementById('signin-error');\n  \n  try {\n    const response = 
await fetch('/api/auth/signin', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 
'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ email, 
password })\n    });\n    \n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const data = await 
response.json();\n      errorDiv.textContent = data.error || 'Sign-in failed';\n
errorDiv.style.display = 'block';\n      return;\n    }\n    \n    const data = 
await response.json();\n    gameState.token = data.token;\n    gameState.user = 
data.user;\n    gameState.lastRevision = 0;\n    \n    
localStorage.setItem('brickfall_token', data.token);\n    
localStorage.setItem('brickfall_user', JSON.stringify(data.user));\n    \n    
errorDiv.style.display = 'none';\n    showGameScreen();\n  } catch (err) {\n    
console.error('Sign-in error:', err);\n    errorDiv.textContent = 'Network 
error. Please try again.';\n    errorDiv.style.display = 'block';\n  
}\n}\n\nasync function handleSignout() {\n  if (!confirm('Sign out?')) return;\n
\n  try {\n    await apiFetch('/api/auth/signout', { method: 'POST' });\n  } 
catch (err) {\n    console.error('Signout error:', err);\n  }\n  \n  
gameState.token = null;\n  gameState.user = null;\n  gameState.currentRun = 
null;\n  \n  localStorage.removeItem('brickfall_token');\n  
localStorage.removeItem('brickfall_user');\n  
localStorage.removeItem('brickfall_run');\n  \n  
showSigninScreen();\n}\n\nfunction restoreSession() {\n  const token = 
localStorage.getItem('brickfall_token');\n  const user = 
localStorage.getItem('brickfall_user');\n  \n  if (token && user) {\n    
gameState.token = token;\n    gameState.user = JSON.parse(user);\n    
showGameScreen();\n  }\n}\n\n// ============ SCREEN MANAGEMENT 
============\n\nfunction showSigninScreen() {\n  
document.getElementById('signin-screen').classList.add('active');\n  
document.getElementById('game-screen').classList.remove('active');\n}\n\nfunctio
n showGameScreen() {\n  
document.getElementById('signin-screen').classList.remove('active');\n  
document.getElementById('game-screen').classList.add('active');\n  \n  
updatePlayerInfo();\n  loadLevels();\n  loadLeaderboard();\n  
loadRunHistory();\n  loadDrills();\n  loadActiveRun();\n}\n\nfunction 
updatePlayerInfo() {\n  if (!gameState.user) return;\n  \n  
document.querySelector('.player-name').textContent = gameState.user.name;\n  
document.querySelector('.player-initials').textContent = 
gameState.user.initials;\n}\n\n// ============ LEVELS ============\n\nasync 
function loadLevels() {\n  try {\n    const data = await 
apiFetch('/api/levels');\n    const container = 
document.getElementById('levels-list');\n    container.innerHTML = '';\n    \n  
data.all.forEach(level => {\n      const btn = 
document.createElement('button');\n      btn.className = 'level-btn';\n      
btn.textContent = `Level ${level.level}: ${level.name}`;\n      
btn.style.borderLeftColor = level.accent;\n      btn.style.borderLeftWidth = 
'3px';\n      \n      if (level.level > data.unlocked) {\n        
btn.classList.add('locked');\n        btn.disabled = true;\n        
btn.textContent += ' (Locked)';\n      } else {\n        
btn.addEventListener('click', () => startNewRun(level.level));\n      }\n      
\n      container.appendChild(btn);\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    
console.error('Failed to load levels:', err);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function 
startNewRun(level) {\n  if (gameState.currentRun && gameState.gamePhase !== 
'game-over' && gameState.gamePhase !== 'completed') {\n    if (!confirm('Abandon
current run?')) return;\n  }\n  \n  const operationId = generateOperationId();\n
gameState.activeOperationId = operationId;\n  \n  try {\n    const response = 
await apiFetch('/api/game/start', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: 
JSON.stringify({\n        level,\n        revision: gameState.lastRevision,\n   
operationId\n      })\n    });\n    \n    if (!response.success) {\n      if 
(response.status === 409) {\n        gameState.lastRevision = 
response.revision;\n        loadActiveRun();\n        return;\n      }\n      
showStatus(response.error || 'Failed to start run', 'error');\n      return;\n  
}\n    \n    gameState.lastRevision = response.revision;\n    
gameState.currentRun = response.run;\n    gameState.currentLevel = level;\n    
gameState.gamePhase = 'ready';\n    \n    initializeGameState();\n    
showStatus(`Level ${level} loaded. Press Space to launch!`, 'info');\n  } catch 
(err) {\n    console.error('Failed to start run:', err);\n    showStatus('Failed
to start run', 'error');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function loadActiveRun() {\n  try {\n 
const data = await apiFetch('/api/game/state');\n    \n    if (data.activeRun) 
{\n      gameState.currentRun = data.activeRun;\n      gameState.currentLevel = 
data.activeRun.level;\n      gameState.lastRevision = data.activeRun.revision;\n
gameState.gamePhase = 'paused';\n      \n      
document.getElementById('resume-run-btn').style.display = 'block';\n      
document.getElementById('resume-run-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {\n   
gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';\n        startGameLoop();\n      });\n      \n 
updateHUD();\n    }\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error('Failed to load active
run:', err);\n  }\n}\n\n// ============ GAME INITIALIZATION 
============\n\nfunction initializeGameState() {\n  if (!gameState.currentRun) 
{\n    gameState.currentRun = {\n      state: {\n        level: 
gameState.currentLevel,\n        score: 0,\n        lives: 3,\n        combo: 
1,\n        nextExtraLife: 20000,\n        balls: [{ x: 400, y: 400, vx: 0, vy: 
0, stuck: true }],\n        paddle: { x: 300, y: 720, width: 118, height: 15, 
recentMotion: 0 },\n        bricks: [],\n        drops: [],\n        power: 
null,\n        powerSeconds: 0\n      }\n    };\n  }\n  \n  loadLevelBricks();\n
gameState.gamePhase = 'ready';\n  updateHUD();\n}\n\nasync function 
loadLevelBricks() {\n  try {\n    const levelNum = gameState.currentLevel || 
1;\n    // Fetch bricks for this level\n    // For now, initialize with 
placeholder\n    gameState.currentRun.state.bricks = 
generateBricksForLevel(levelNum);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error('Failed 
to load bricks:', err);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction generateBricksForLevel(level) {\n  
// Generate a simple brick pattern\n  // This should be fetched from the 
database in a real scenario\n  const bricks = [];\n  const colors = ['normal', 
'strong', 'solid'];\n  const rows = 4;\n  const cols = 8;\n  \n  for (let r = 0;
r < rows; r++) {\n    for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {\n      const type = 
colors[r % colors.length];\n      bricks.push({\n        x: c * 100,\n        y:
r * 40 + 60,\n        w: 95,\n        h: 35,\n        type,\n        destroyed: 
false,\n        damaged: false,\n        drop: null\n      });\n    }\n  }\n  \n
return bricks;\n}\n\n// ============ GAME LOOP ============\n\nlet 
animationFrameId = null;\nlet lastFrameTime = 0;\nlet physicsAccumulator = 
0;\n\nasync function startGameLoop() {\n  gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';\n  \n
function loop(currentTime) {\n    if (!lastFrameTime) lastFrameTime = 
currentTime;\n    const deltaTime = Math.min((currentTime - lastFrameTime) / 
1000, 0.016);\n    lastFrameTime = currentTime;\n    \n    if 
(gameState.gamePhase === 'playing') {\n      physicsAccumulator += deltaTime;\n 
\n      while (physicsAccumulator >= PHYSICS_STEP) {\n        updatePhysics();\n
physicsAccumulator -= PHYSICS_STEP;\n      }\n      \n      const now = 
Date.now();\n      if (now - gameState.lastSaveTime > SAVE_INTERVAL) {\n        
saveGameState();\n        gameState.lastSaveTime = now;\n      }\n    }\n    \n 
render();\n    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n  }\n  \n  
animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n}\n\nfunction updatePhysics() 
{\n  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;\n  \n  const state = 
gameState.currentRun.state;\n  const paddle = inputState.paddle;\n  \n  // 
Update paddle position based on input\n  if (inputState.left && paddle.x > 0) 
{\n    paddle.x -= 350 * PHYSICS_STEP;\n    paddle.recentMotion = -350;\n  } 
else if (inputState.right && paddle.x < GAME_WIDTH - paddle.width) {\n    
paddle.x += 350 * PHYSICS_STEP;\n    paddle.recentMotion = 350;\n  } else {\n   
paddle.recentMotion = 0;\n  }\n  \n  // Move stuck balls with paddle\n  
state.balls.forEach(ball => {\n    if (ball.stuck) {\n      ball.x = paddle.x + 
paddle.width / 2;\n      ball.y = paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5;\n    }\n  });\n  
\n  // Update power-up timer\n  if (state.power && state.powerSeconds > 0) {\n  
state.powerSeconds -= PHYSICS_STEP;\n    if (state.powerSeconds <= 0) {\n      
state.powerSeconds = 0;\n      // Reset power-up effects\n      if (state.power 
=== 'wide') {\n        inputState.paddle.width = 118;\n      } else if 
(state.power === 'slow') {\n        state.balls.forEach(ball => {\n          
const speed = Math.sqrt(ball.vx ** 2 + ball.vy ** 2);\n          if (speed > 0) 
{\n            ball.vx /= 0.7;\n            ball.vy /= 0.7;\n          }\n      
});\n      }\n      state.power = null;\n    }\n  }\n  \n  // Update drops\n  
const newDrops = [];\n  state.drops.forEach(drop => {\n    drop.y += drop.vy * 
PHYSICS_STEP;\n    \n    if (drop.y < GAME_HEIGHT) {\n      // Check paddle 
collision\n      if (drop.y + 8 > paddle.y - 15 && drop.y - 8 < paddle.y &&\n   
drop.x > paddle.x && drop.x < paddle.x + paddle.width) {\n        
applyPowerUp(drop.type);\n        return;\n      }\n      newDrops.push(drop);\n
}\n  });\n  state.drops = newDrops;\n  \n  // Simulate ball physics 
(simplified)\n  state.balls.forEach((ball, idx) => {\n    if (ball.stuck || 
ball.lost) return;\n    \n    // Move ball\n    ball.x += ball.vx * 
PHYSICS_STEP;\n    ball.y += ball.vy * PHYSICS_STEP;\n    \n    // Wall 
bounces\n    if (ball.x - BALL_RADIUS < 0) {\n      ball.x = BALL_RADIUS;\n     
ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx);\n    }\n    if (ball.x + BALL_RADIUS > GAME_WIDTH) 
{\n      ball.x = GAME_WIDTH - BALL_RADIUS;\n      ball.vx = 
-Math.abs(ball.vx);\n    }\n    if (ball.y - BALL_RADIUS < 0) {\n      ball.y = 
BALL_RADIUS;\n      ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy);\n    }\n    \n    // Check if 
lost\n    if (ball.y > GAME_HEIGHT + 50) {\n      ball.lost = true;\n      
state.lives--;\n      \n      if (state.lives > 0) {\n        
gameState.gamePhase = 'life-lost';\n        showStatus(`Ball lost! 
${state.lives} lives remaining`, 'error');\n        // Reset after 2 seconds\n  
setTimeout(() => {\n          resetBall();\n          gameState.gamePhase = 
'playing';\n        }, 2000);\n      } else {\n        gameState.gamePhase = 
'game-over';\n        finishRun('game-over');\n      }\n    }\n    \n    // 
Paddle collision\n    const paddleRect = {\n      x: paddle.x,\n      y: 
paddle.y - 15,\n      w: paddle.width,\n      h: 15\n    };\n    \n    if 
(circleRectCollision(ball, paddleRect)) {\n      ball.y = paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS
- 5;\n      ball.vy = -Math.abs(ball.vy);\n      state.combo = 1;\n    }\n    \n
// Brick collision\n    for (let i = 0; i < state.bricks.length; i++) {\n      
const brick = state.bricks[i];\n      if (brick.destroyed) continue;\n      \n  
const brickRect = { x: brick.x, y: brick.y, w: brick.w, h: brick.h };\n      \n 
if (circleRectCollision(ball, brickRect)) {\n        ball.vy = -ball.vy;\n      
\n        if (brick.type !== 'solid') {\n          if (brick.type === 'normal') 
{\n            brick.destroyed = true;\n            state.score += 100 * 
state.combo;\n            if (brick.drop) {\n              state.drops.push({\n 
type: brick.drop,\n                x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,\n                y:
brick.y + brick.h / 2,\n                vy: 100\n              });\n            
}\n            state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);\n          } else if 
(brick.type === 'strong') {\n            if (!brick.damaged) {\n              
brick.damaged = true;\n              state.score += 75 * state.combo;\n         
state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);\n            } else {\n             
brick.destroyed = true;\n              state.score += 250 * state.combo;\n      
if (brick.drop) {\n                state.drops.push({\n                  type: 
brick.drop,\n                  x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,\n                  y: 
brick.y + brick.h / 2,\n                  vy: 100\n                });\n        
}\n              state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);\n            }\n   
}\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  });\n  \n  // Check level completion\n  const 
breakableDestroyed = state.bricks.every(b => b.type === 'solid' || 
b.destroyed);\n  if (breakableDestroyed && state.balls.some(b => !b.lost)) {\n  
state.score += 1000 * gameState.currentLevel;\n    gameState.gamePhase = 
'level-complete';\n    \n    if (gameState.currentLevel === 10) {\n      
gameState.gamePhase = 'completed';\n      finishRun('completed');\n    } else 
{\n      showStatus(`Level ${gameState.currentLevel} completed!`, 'success');\n 
setTimeout(() => {\n        gameState.currentLevel++;\n        
initializeGameState();\n        gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';\n      }, 
2000);\n    }\n  }\n  \n  updateHUD();\n}\n\nfunction 
circleRectCollision(circle, rect) {\n  const closestX = Math.max(rect.x, 
Math.min(circle.x, rect.x + rect.w));\n  const closestY = Math.max(rect.y, 
Math.min(circle.y, rect.y + rect.h));\n  \n  const dx = circle.x - closestX;\n  
const dy = circle.y - closestY;\n  \n  return (dx * dx + dy * dy) < (BALL_RADIUS
* BALL_RADIUS);\n}\n\nfunction resetBall() {\n  if (!gameState.currentRun) 
return;\n  \n  const state = gameState.currentRun.state;\n  const paddle = 
inputState.paddle;\n  \n  state.balls = [{\n    x: paddle.x + paddle.width / 
2,\n    y: paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5,\n    vx: 0,\n    vy: 0,\n    stuck: 
true,\n    lost: false\n  }];\n  \n  showStatus('Press Space to launch', 
'info');\n}\n\nasync function saveGameState() {\n  if (!gameState.currentRun || 
gameState.gamePhase === 'menu') return;\n  \n  try {\n    await 
apiFetch('/api/game/save', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: 
JSON.stringify({\n        state: gameState.currentRun.state,\n        revision: 
gameState.lastRevision,\n        operationId: generateOperationId()\n      })\n 
});\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error('Failed to save:', err);\n  
}\n}\n\nasync function finishRun(outcome) {\n  if (!gameState.currentRun) 
return;\n  \n  try {\n    const response = await apiFetch('/api/game/finish', 
{\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        outcome,\n      
level: gameState.currentLevel,\n        score: 
gameState.currentRun.state.score,\n        snapshot: 
gameState.currentRun.state,\n        revision: gameState.lastRevision,\n        
operationId: generateOperationId()\n      })\n    });\n    \n    if 
(response.success) {\n      showStatus(`Run ${outcome}! Score: 
${gameState.currentRun.state.score}`, 'success');\n      gameState.currentRun = 
null;\n      gameState.lastRevision = 0;\n      loadLeaderboard();\n      
loadRunHistory();\n      gameState.gamePhase = outcome === 'completed' ? 
'completed' : 'game-over';\n    }\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error('Failed 
to finish run:', err);\n  }\n}\n\n// ============ RENDERING 
============\n\nfunction render() {\n  const canvas = 
document.getElementById('game-canvas');\n  const ctx = 
canvas.getContext('2d');\n  \n  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0e27';\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0,
GAME_WIDTH, GAME_HEIGHT);\n  \n  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;\n  \n  const
state = gameState.currentRun.state;\n  \n  // Draw bricks\n  
state.bricks.forEach(brick => {\n    if (brick.destroyed) return;\n    \n    if 
(brick.type === 'normal') {\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';\n    } else if 
(brick.type === 'strong') {\n      ctx.fillStyle = brick.damaged ? '#7ee787' : 
'#ff6b6b';\n    } else {\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#4a5568';\n    }\n    \n    
ctx.fillRect(brick.x, brick.y, brick.w, brick.h);\n    ctx.strokeStyle = 
'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)';\n    ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n    ctx.strokeRect(brick.x,
brick.y, brick.w, brick.h);\n  });\n  \n  // Draw paddle\n  const paddle = 
inputState.paddle;\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';\n  ctx.fillRect(paddle.x, 
paddle.y - 15, paddle.width, 15);\n  \n  // Draw balls\n  ctx.fillStyle = 
'#fff';\n  state.balls.forEach(ball => {\n    if (ball.lost) return;\n    
ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n   
ctx.fill();\n  });\n  \n  // Draw drops\n  state.drops.forEach(drop => {\n    
const colors = {\n      wide: '#ff9999',\n      slow: '#99ff99',\n      
multiball: '#ffff99',\n      sticky: '#ff99ff'\n    };\n    ctx.fillStyle = 
colors[drop.type] || '#ffffff';\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(drop.x, 
drop.y, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n  });\n}\n\n// ============ HUD 
UPDATE ============\n\nfunction updateHUD() {\n  if (!gameState.currentRun) 
return;\n  \n  const state = gameState.currentRun.state;\n  \n  
document.getElementById('score-display').textContent = state.score;\n  
document.getElementById('level-display').textContent = gameState.currentLevel ||
1;\n  document.getElementById('lives-display').textContent = state.lives;\n  
document.getElementById('combo-display').textContent = `\u00d7${state.combo}`;\n
\n  if (state.power) {\n    
document.getElementById('power-display').style.display = 'block';\n    
document.getElementById('power-name').textContent = state.power;\n    
document.getElementById('power-timer').textContent = 
`${Math.ceil(state.powerSeconds)}s`;\n  } else {\n    
document.getElementById('power-display').style.display = 'none';\n  
}\n}\n\nfunction showStatus(message, type = 'info') {\n  const el = 
document.getElementById('status-message');\n  el.textContent = message;\n  
el.className = `status-message ${type}`;\n}\n\n// ============ INPUT HANDLING 
============\n\nfunction handleKeyDown(e) {\n  if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || 
e.key === 'a') {\n    inputState.left = true;\n    if (gameState.currentRun && 
inputState.paddle) {\n      
document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;\n    }\n  }\n  
if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') {\n    inputState.right = true;\n  
if (gameState.currentRun && inputState.paddle) {\n      
document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;\n    }\n  }\n  
if (e.key === ' ') {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    launchBall();\n  }\n  if 
(e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P') {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    
togglePause();\n  }\n  if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    
togglePause();\n  }\n  if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {\n    
e.preventDefault();\n    if (gameState.gamePhase === 'game-over' || 
gameState.gamePhase === 'completed') {\n      restartRun();\n    }\n  
}\n}\n\nfunction handleKeyUp(e) {\n  if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a')
inputState.left = false;\n  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') 
inputState.right = false;\n}\n\nfunction handleCanvasMouseMove(e) {\n  const 
canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');\n  const rect = 
canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n  \n  if 
(gameState.currentRun) {\n    inputState.paddle.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x - 
inputState.paddle.width / 2, GAME_WIDTH - inputState.paddle.width));\n    
document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;\n  
}\n}\n\nfunction handleCanvasTouchMove(e) {\n  const canvas = 
document.getElementById('game-canvas');\n  const rect = 
canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const touch = e.touches[0];\n  const x = 
touch.clientX - rect.left;\n  \n  if (gameState.currentRun) {\n    
inputState.paddle.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x - inputState.paddle.width / 2, 
GAME_WIDTH - inputState.paddle.width));\n    
document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;\n  
}\n}\n\nfunction launchBall() {\n  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;\n  \n  
const state = gameState.currentRun.state;\n  const stuck = state.balls.find(b =>
b.stuck);\n  \n  if (stuck) {\n    stuck.stuck = false;\n    stuck.vx = 
(Math.random() - 0.5) * 400;\n    stuck.vy = -300;\n    gameState.gamePhase = 
'playing';\n    if (!animationFrameId) startGameLoop();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction 
togglePause() {\n  if (gameState.gamePhase === 'playing') {\n    
gameState.gamePhase = 'paused';\n    showStatus('Paused', 'info');\n  } else if 
(gameState.gamePhase === 'paused') {\n    gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';\n    
if (!animationFrameId) startGameLoop();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction restartRun() {\n  if
(gameState.currentLevel) {\n    gameState.currentRun = null;\n    
startNewRun(gameState.currentLevel);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction applyPowerUp(type) {\n 
if (!gameState.currentRun) return;\n  \n  const state = 
gameState.currentRun.state;\n  \n  if (type === 'wide') {\n    
inputState.paddle.width = Math.min(177, inputState.paddle.width * 1.5);\n  } 
else if (type === 'slow') {\n    state.balls.forEach(ball => {\n      if 
(!ball.stuck) {\n        ball.vx *= 0.7;\n        ball.vy *= 0.7;\n      }\n    
});\n  } else if (type === 'multiball') {\n    const moving = state.balls.find(b
=> !b.stuck && !b.lost);\n    if (moving && state.balls.length < 2) {\n      
state.balls.push({\n        x: moving.x - 20,\n        y: moving.y - 20,\n      
vx: moving.vx * 0.8,\n        vy: moving.vy * 0.8,\n        stuck: false,\n     
lost: false\n      });\n    }\n  } else if (type === 'sticky') {\n    const 
moving = state.balls.find(b => !b.stuck && !b.lost);\n    if (moving) {\n      
moving.stuck = true;\n    }\n  }\n  \n  if (state.power === type) {\n

    // ============ AUTHENTICATION ============
    
    async function handleSignin(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
      const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
      const errorDiv = document.getElementById('signin-error');
      
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/auth/signin', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
        });
        
        if (!response.ok) {
          const data = await response.json();
          errorDiv.textContent = data.error || 'Sign-in failed';
          errorDiv.style.display = 'block';
          return;
        }
        
        const data = await response.json();
        gameState.token = data.token;
        gameState.user = data.user;
        gameState.lastRevision = 0;
        
        localStorage.setItem('brickfall_token', data.token);
        localStorage.setItem('brickfall_user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
        
        errorDiv.style.display = 'none';
        showGameScreen();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Sign-in error:', err);
        errorDiv.textContent = 'Network error. Please try again.';
        errorDiv.style.display = 'block';
      }
    }
    
    async function handleSignout() {
      if (!confirm('Sign out?')) return;
      
      try {
        await apiFetch('/api/auth/signout', { method: 'POST' });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Signout error:', err);
      }
      
      gameState.token = null;
      gameState.user = null;
      gameState.currentRun = null;
      
      localStorage.removeItem('brickfall_token');
      localStorage.removeItem('brickfall_user');
      localStorage.removeItem('brickfall_run');
      
      showSigninScreen();
    }
    
    function restoreSession() {
      const token = localStorage.getItem('brickfall_token');
      const user = localStorage.getItem('brickfall_user');
      
      if (token && user) {
        gameState.token = token;
        gameState.user = JSON.parse(user);
        showGameScreen();
      }
    }
    
    // ============ SCREEN MANAGEMENT ============
    
    function showSigninScreen() {
      document.getElementById('signin-screen').classList.add('active');
      document.getElementById('game-screen').classList.remove('active');
    }
    
    function showGameScreen() {
      document.getElementById('signin-screen').classList.remove('active');
      document.getElementById('game-screen').classList.add('active');
      
      updatePlayerInfo();
      loadLevels();
      loadLeaderboard();
      loadRunHistory();
      loadDrills();
      loadActiveRun();
    }
    
    function updatePlayerInfo() {
      if (!gameState.user) return;
      
      document.querySelector('.player-name').textContent = gameState.user.name;
      document.querySelector('.player-initials').textContent = 
gameState.user.initials;
    }
    
    // ============ LEVELS ============
    
    async function loadLevels() {
      try {
        const data = await apiFetch('/api/levels');
        const container = document.getElementById('levels-list');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        
        data.all.forEach(level => {
          const btn = document.createElement('button');
          btn.className = 'level-btn';
          btn.textContent = `Level ${level.level}: ${level.name}`;
          btn.style.borderLeftColor = level.accent;
          btn.style.borderLeftWidth = '3px';
          
          if (level.level > data.unlocked) {
            btn.classList.add('locked');
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.textContent += ' (Locked)';
          } else {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => startNewRun(level.level));
          }
          
          container.appendChild(btn);
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load levels:', err);
      }
    }
    
    async function startNewRun(level) {
      if (gameState.currentRun && gameState.gamePhase !== 'game-over' && 
gameState.gamePhase !== 'completed') {
        if (!confirm('Abandon current run?')) return;
      }
      
      const operationId = generateOperationId();
      gameState.activeOperationId = operationId;
      
      try {
        const response = await apiFetch('/api/game/start', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            level,
            revision: gameState.lastRevision,
            operationId
          })
        });
        
        if (!response.success) {
          if (response.status === 409) {
            gameState.lastRevision = response.revision;
            loadActiveRun();
            return;
          }
          showStatus(response.error || 'Failed to start run', 'error');
          return;
        }
        
        gameState.lastRevision = response.revision;
        gameState.currentRun = response.run;
        gameState.currentLevel = level;
        gameState.gamePhase = 'ready';
        
        initializeGameState();
        showStatus(`Level ${level} loaded. Press Space to launch!`, 'info');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to start run:', err);
        showStatus('Failed to start run', 'error');
      }
    }
    
    async function loadActiveRun() {
      try {
        const data = await apiFetch('/api/game/state');
        
        if (data.activeRun) {
          gameState.currentRun = data.activeRun;
          gameState.currentLevel = data.activeRun.level;
          gameState.lastRevision = data.activeRun.revision;
          gameState.gamePhase = 'paused';
          
          document.getElementById('resume-run-btn').style.display = 'block';
          document.getElementById('resume-run-btn').addEventListener('click', ()
=> {
            gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
            startGameLoop();
          });
          
          updateHUD();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load active run:', err);
      }
    }
    
    // ============ GAME INITIALIZATION ============
    
    function initializeGameState() {
      if (!gameState.currentRun) {
        gameState.currentRun = {
          state: {
            level: gameState.currentLevel,
            score: 0,
            lives: 3,
            combo: 1,
            nextExtraLife: 20000,
            balls: [{ x: 400, y: 400, vx: 0, vy: 0, stuck: true }],
            paddle: { x: 300, y: 720, width: 118, height: 15, recentMotion: 0 },
            bricks: [],
            drops: [],
            power: null,
            powerSeconds: 0
          }
        };
      }
      
      loadLevelBricks();
      gameState.gamePhase = 'ready';
      updateHUD();
    }
    
    async function loadLevelBricks() {
      try {
        const levelNum = gameState.currentLevel || 1;
        // Fetch bricks for this level
        // For now, initialize with placeholder
        gameState.currentRun.state.bricks = generateBricksForLevel(levelNum);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load bricks:', err);
      }
    }
    
    function generateBricksForLevel(level) {
      // Generate a simple brick pattern
      // This should be fetched from the database in a real scenario
      const bricks = [];
      const colors = ['normal', 'strong', 'solid'];
      const rows = 4;
      const cols = 8;
      
      for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
          const type = colors[r % colors.length];
          bricks.push({
            x: c * 100,
            y: r * 40 + 60,
            w: 95,
            h: 35,
            type,
            destroyed: false,
            damaged: false,
            drop: null
          });
        }
      }
      
      return bricks;
    }
    
    // ============ GAME LOOP ============
    
    let animationFrameId = null;
    let lastFrameTime = 0;
    let physicsAccumulator = 0;
    
    async function startGameLoop() {
      gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
      
      function loop(currentTime) {
        if (!lastFrameTime) lastFrameTime = currentTime;
        const deltaTime = Math.min((currentTime - lastFrameTime) / 1000, 0.016);
        lastFrameTime = currentTime;
        
        if (gameState.gamePhase === 'playing') {
          physicsAccumulator += deltaTime;
          
          while (physicsAccumulator >= PHYSICS_STEP) {
            updatePhysics();
            physicsAccumulator -= PHYSICS_STEP;
          }
          
          const now = Date.now();
          if (now - gameState.lastSaveTime > SAVE_INTERVAL) {
            saveGameState();
            gameState.lastSaveTime = now;
          }
        }
        
        render();
        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
      }
      
      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    }
    
    function updatePhysics() {
      if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
      
      const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
      const paddle = inputState.paddle;
      
      // Update paddle position based on input
      if (inputState.left && paddle.x > 0) {
        paddle.x -= 350 * PHYSICS_STEP;
        paddle.recentMotion = -350;
      } else if (inputState.right && paddle.x < GAME_WIDTH - paddle.width) {
        paddle.x += 350 * PHYSICS_STEP;
        paddle.recentMotion = 350;
      } else {
        paddle.recentMotion = 0;
      }
      
      // Move stuck balls with paddle
      state.balls.forEach(ball => {
        if (ball.stuck) {
          ball.x = paddle.x + paddle.width / 2;
          ball.y = paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5;
        }
      });
      
      // Update power-up timer
      if (state.power && state.powerSeconds > 0) {
        state.powerSeconds -= PHYSICS_STEP;
        if (state.powerSeconds <= 0) {
          state.powerSeconds = 0;
          // Reset power-up effects
          if (state.power === 'wide') {
            inputState.paddle.width = 118;
          } else if (state.power === 'slow') {
            state.balls.forEach(ball => {
              const speed = Math.sqrt(ball.vx ** 2 + ball.vy ** 2);
              if (speed > 0) {
                ball.vx /= 0.7;
                ball.vy /= 0.7;
              }
            });
          }
          state.power = null;
        }
      }
      
      // Update drops
      const newDrops = [];
      state.drops.forEach(drop => {
        drop.y += drop.vy * PHYSICS_STEP;
        
        if (drop.y < GAME_HEIGHT) {
          // Check paddle collision
          if (drop.y + 8 > paddle.y - 15 && drop.y - 8 < paddle.y &&
              drop.x > paddle.x && drop.x < paddle.x + paddle.width) {
            applyPowerUp(drop.type);
            return;
          }
          newDrops.push(drop);
        }
      });
      state.drops = newDrops;
      
      // Simulate ball physics (simplified)
      state.balls.forEach((ball, idx) => {
        if (ball.stuck || ball.lost) return;
        
        // Move ball
        ball.x += ball.vx * PHYSICS_STEP;
        ball.y += ball.vy * PHYSICS_STEP;
        
        // Wall bounces
        if (ball.x - BALL_RADIUS < 0) {
          ball.x = BALL_RADIUS;
          ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx);
        }
        if (ball.x + BALL_RADIUS > GAME_WIDTH) {
          ball.x = GAME_WIDTH - BALL_RADIUS;
          ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx);
        }
        if (ball.y - BALL_RADIUS < 0) {
          ball.y = BALL_RADIUS;
          ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy);
        }
        
        // Check if lost
        if (ball.y > GAME_HEIGHT + 50) {
          ball.lost = true;
          state.lives--;
          
          if (state.lives > 0) {
            gameState.gamePhase = 'life-lost';
            showStatus(`Ball lost! ${state.lives} lives remaining`, 'error');
            // Reset after 2 seconds
            setTimeout(() => {
              resetBall();
              gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
            }, 2000);
          } else {
            gameState.gamePhase = 'game-over';
            finishRun('game-over');
          }
        }
        
        // Paddle collision
        const paddleRect = {
          x: paddle.x,
          y: paddle.y - 15,
          w: paddle.width,
          h: 15
        };
        
        if (circleRectCollision(ball, paddleRect)) {
          ball.y = paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5;
          ball.vy = -Math.abs(ball.vy);
          state.combo = 1;
        }
        
        // Brick collision
        for (let i = 0; i < state.bricks.length; i++) {
          const brick = state.bricks[i];
          if (brick.destroyed) continue;
          
          const brickRect = { x: brick.x, y: brick.y, w: brick.w, h: brick.h };
          
          if (circleRectCollision(ball, brickRect)) {
            ball.vy = -ball.vy;
            
            if (brick.type !== 'solid') {
              if (brick.type === 'normal') {
                brick.destroyed = true;
                state.score += 100 * state.combo;
                if (brick.drop) {
                  state.drops.push({
                    type: brick.drop,
                    x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,
                    y: brick.y + brick.h / 2,
                    vy: 100
                  });
                }
                state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);
              } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
                if (!brick.damaged) {
                  brick.damaged = true;
                  state.score += 75 * state.combo;
                  state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);
                } else {
                  brick.destroyed = true;
                  state.score += 250 * state.combo;
                  if (brick.drop) {
                    state.drops.push({
                      type: brick.drop,
                      x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,
                      y: brick.y + brick.h / 2,
                      vy: 100
                    });
                  }
                  state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      });
      
      // Check level completion
      const breakableDestroyed = state.bricks.every(b => b.type === 'solid' || 
b.destroyed);
      if (breakableDestroyed && state.balls.some(b => !b.lost)) {
        state.score += 1000 * gameState.currentLevel;
        gameState.gamePhase = 'level-complete';
        
        if (gameState.currentLevel === 10) {
          gameState.gamePhase = 'completed';
          finishRun('completed');
        } else {
          showStatus(`Level ${gameState.currentLevel} completed!`, 'success');
          setTimeout(() => {
            gameState.currentLevel++;
            initializeGameState();
            gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
          }, 2000);
        }
      }
      
      updateHUD();
    }
    
    function circleRectCollision(circle, rect) {
      const closestX = Math.max(rect.x, Math.min(circle.x, rect.x + rect.w));
      const closestY = Math.max(rect.y, Math.min(circle.y, rect.y + rect.h));
      
      const dx = circle.x - closestX;
      const dy = circle.y - closestY;
      
      return (dx * dx + dy * dy) < (BALL_RADIUS * BALL_RADIUS);
    }
    
    function resetBall() {
      if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
      
      const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
      const paddle = inputState.paddle;
      
      state.balls = [{
        x: paddle.x + paddle.width / 2,
        y: paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5,
        vx: 0,
        vy: 0,
        stuck: true,
        lost: false
      }];
      
      showStatus('Press Space to launch', 'info');
    }
    
    async function saveGameState() {
      if (!gameState.currentRun || gameState.gamePhase === 'menu') return;
      
      try {
        await apiFetch('/api/game/save', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            state: gameState.currentRun.state,
            revision: gameState.lastRevision,
            operationId: generateOperationId()
          })
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to save:', err);
      }
    }
    
    async function finishRun(outcome) {
      if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
      
      try {
        const response = await apiFetch('/api/game/finish', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            outcome,
            level: gameState.currentLevel,
            score: gameState.currentRun.state.score,
            snapshot: gameState.currentRun.state,
            revision: gameState.lastRevision,
            operationId: generateOperationId()
          })
        });
        
        if (response.success) {
          showStatus(`Run ${outcome}! Score: 
${gameState.currentRun.state.score}`, 'success');
          gameState.currentRun = null;
          gameState.lastRevision = 0;
          loadLeaderboard();
          loadRunHistory();
          gameState.gamePhase = outcome === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 
'game-over';
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to finish run:', err);
      }
    }
    
    // ============ RENDERING ============
    
    function render() {
      const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0e27';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, GAME_WIDTH, GAME_HEIGHT);
      
      if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
      
      const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
      
      // Draw bricks
      state.bricks.forEach(brick => {
        if (brick.destroyed) return;
        
        if (brick.type === 'normal') {
          ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';
        } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
          ctx.fillStyle = brick.damaged ? '#7ee787' : '#ff6b6b';
        } else {
          ctx.fillStyle = '#4a5568';
        }
        
        ctx.fillRect(brick.x, brick.y, brick.w, brick.h);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.strokeRect(brick.x, brick.y, brick.w, brick.h);
      });
      
      // Draw paddle
      const paddle = inputState.paddle;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';
      ctx.fillRect(paddle.x, paddle.y - 15, paddle.width, 15);
      
      // Draw balls
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
      state.balls.forEach(ball => {
        if (ball.lost) return;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      });
      
      // Draw drops
      state.drops.forEach(drop => {
        const colors = {
          wide: '#ff9999',
          slow: '#99ff99',
          multiball: '#ffff99',
          sticky: '#ff99ff'
        };
        ctx.fillStyle = colors[drop.type] || '#ffffff';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(drop.x, drop.y, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      });
    }
    
    // ============ HUD UPDATE ============
    
    function updateHUD() {
      if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
      
      const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
      
      document.getElementById('score-display').textContent = state.score;
      document.getElementById('level-display').textContent = 
gameState.currentLevel || 1;
      document.getElementById('lives-display').textContent = state.lives;
      document.getElementById('combo-display').textContent = `×${state.combo}`;
      
      if (state.power) {
        document.getElementById('power-display').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('power-name').textContent = state.power;
        document.getElementById('power-timer').textContent = 
`${Math.ceil(state.powerSeconds)}s`;
      } else {
        document.getElementById('power-display').style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
    
    function showStatus(message, type = 'info') {
      const el = document.getElementById('status-message');
      el.textContent = message;
      el.className = `status-message ${type}`;
    }
    
    // ============ INPUT HANDLING ============
    
    function handleKeyDown(e) {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a') {
        inputState.left = true;
        if (gameState.currentRun && inputState.paddle) {
          document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
        }
      }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') {
        inputState.right = true;
        if (gameState.currentRun && inputState.paddle) {
          document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
        }
      }
      if (e.key === ' ') {
        e.preventDefault();
        launchBall();
      }
      if (e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P') {
        e.preventDefault();
        togglePause();
      }
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        togglePause();
      }
      if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (gameState.gamePhase === 'game-over' || gameState.gamePhase === 
'completed') {
          restartRun();
        }
      }
    }
    
    function handleKeyUp(e) {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a') inputState.left = false;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') inputState.right = false;
    }
    
    function handleCanvasMouseMove(e) {
      const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
      
      if (gameState.currentRun) {
        inputState.paddle.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x - inputState.paddle.width /
2, GAME_WIDTH - inputState.paddle.width));
        document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
      }
    }
    
    function handleCanvasTouchMove(e) {
      const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const touch = e.touches[0];
      const x = touch.clientX - rect.left;
      
      if (gameState.currentRun) {
        inputState.paddle.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x - inputState.paddle.width /
2, GAME_WIDTH - inputState.paddle.width));
        document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
      }
    }
    
    function launchBall() {
      if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
      
      const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
      const stuck = state.balls.find(b => b.stuck);
      
      if (stuck) {
        stuck.stuck = false;
        stuck.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 400;
        stuck.vy = -300;
        gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
        if (!animationFrameId) startGameLoop();
      }
    }
    
    function togglePause() {
      if (gameState.gamePhase === 'playing') {
        gameState.gamePhase = 'paused';
        showStatus('Paused', 'info');
      } else if (gameState.gamePhase === 'paused') {
        gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
        if (!animationFrameId) startGameLoop();
      }
    }
    
    function restartRun() {
      if (gameState.currentLevel) {
        gameState.currentRun = null;
        startNewRun(gameState.currentLevel);
      }
    }
    
    function applyPowerUp(type) {
      if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
      
      const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
      
      if (type === 'wide') {
        inputState.paddle.width = Math.min(177, inputState.paddle.width * 1.5);
      } else if (type === 'slow') {
        state.balls.forEach(ball => {
          if (!ball.stuck) {
            ball.vx *= 0.7;
            ball.vy *= 0.7;
          }
        });
      } else if (type === 'multiball') {
        const moving = state.balls.find(b => !b.stuck && !b.lost);
        if (moving && state.balls.length < 2) {
          state.balls.push({
            x: moving.x - 20,
            y: moving.y - 20,
            vx: moving.vx * 0.8,
            vy: moving.vy * 0.8,
            stuck: false,
            lost: false
          });
        }
      } else if (type === 'sticky') {
        const moving = state.balls.find(b => !b.stuck && !b.lost);
        if (moving) {
          moving.stuck = true;
        }
      }
      
      if (state.power === type) {
        state.powerSeconds = 20;
      } else {
        state.power = type;
        state.powerSeconds = 20;
      }
      
      showStatus(`${type} collected!`, 'success');
    }
    
    function updateAssistPaddle() {
      // Implement assist paddle logic
    }
    
    // ============ LEADERBOARD & HISTORY ============
    
    async function loadLeaderboard() {
      try {
        const data = await apiFetch('/api/leaderboard');
        const container = document.getElementById('leaderboard-list');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        
        data.leaderboard.forEach((entry, idx) => {

            <div>Score: ${entry.score} | Level ${entry.level}</div>
          `;

      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load leaderboard:', err);
      }
    }

    }
    
    // ============ MECHANICS LAB ============

    async function loadDrill(drillId) {
      try {

        gameState.currentDrill = data.drill;
        gameState.labStepCounter = 0;

        showStatus(`Drill loaded: ${data.drill.name}`, 'success');
      } catch (err) {

        showStatus('Failed to load drill', 'error');
      }
    }
    
    function updateLabTelemetry() {
      if (!gameState.currentDrill) return;
      
      const drill = gameState.currentDrill;
      document.getElementById('lab-phase').textContent = 'paused';
      document.getElementById('lab-score').textContent = '-';
      document.getElementById('lab-lives').textContent = '-';
      document.getElementById('lab-combo').textContent = '-';
      document.getElementById('lab-steps').textContent = 
gameState.labStepCounter;
    }
    
    // ============ TAB NAVIGATION ============
    
    function switchTab(e) {
      const tabName = e.target.getAttribute('data-tab');
      
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.classList.remove('active');
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(content => {
        content.classList.remove('active');
      });
      
      e.target.classList.add('active');
      document.getElementById(`tab-content-${tabName}`).classList.add('active');
    }
    
    // ============ UTILITIES ============
    
    async function apiFetch(url, options = {}) {
      const headers = {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...options.headers
      };
      
      if (gameState.token) {
        headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${gameState.token}`;
      }
      
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        ...options,
        headers
      });
      
      if (response.status === 401) {
        showSigninScreen();
        throw new Error('Unauthorized');
      }
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        const error = await response.json();
        throw new Error(error.error || 'Request failed');
      }
      
      return response.json();
    }
    
    function generateOperationId() {
      return `op-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`;
    }

+332|// ============ GAME LOOP ============
+333|
+334|let animationFrameId = null;
+335|let lastFrameTime = 0;
+336|let physicsAccumulator = 0;
+337|
+338|async function startGameLoop() {
+339|  gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
+340|  
+341|  function loop(currentTime) {
+342|    if (!lastFrameTime) lastFrameTime = currentTime;
+343|    const deltaTime = Math.min((currentTime - lastFrameTime) / 1000, 
0.016);
+344|    lastFrameTime = currentTime;
+345|    
+346|    if (gameState.gamePhase === 'playing') {
+347|      physicsAccumulator += deltaTime;
+348|      
+349|      while (physicsAccumulator >= PHYSICS_STEP) {
+350|        updatePhysics();
+351|        physicsAccumulator -= PHYSICS_STEP;
+352|      }
+353|      
+354|      const now = Date.now();
+355|      if (now - gameState.lastSaveTime > SAVE_INTERVAL) {
+356|        saveGameState();
+357|        gameState.lastSaveTime = now;
+358|      }
+359|    }
+360|    
+361|    render();
+362|    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
+363|  }
+364|  
+365|  animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
+366|}
+367|
+368|function updatePhysics() {
+369|  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
+370|  
+371|  const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
+372|  const paddle = inputState.paddle;
+373|  
+374|  // Update paddle position based on input
+375|  if (inputState.left && paddle.x > 0) {
+376|    paddle.x -= 350 * PHYSICS_STEP;
+377|    paddle.recentMotion = -350;
+378|  } else if (inputState.right && paddle.x < GAME_WIDTH - paddle.width) {
+379|    paddle.x += 350 * PHYSICS_STEP;
+380|    paddle.recentMotion = 350;
+381|  } else {
+382|    paddle.recentMotion = 0;
+383|  }
+384|  
+385|  // Move stuck balls with paddle
+386|  state.balls.forEach(ball => {
+387|    if (ball.stuck) {
+388|      ball.x = paddle.x + paddle.width / 2;
+389|      ball.y = paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5;
+390|    }
+391|  });
+392|  
+393|  // Update power-up timer
+394|  if (state.power && state.powerSeconds > 0) {
+395|    state.powerSeconds -= PHYSICS_STEP;
+396|    if (state.powerSeconds <= 0) {
+397|      state.powerSeconds = 0;
+398|      // Reset power-up effects
+399|      if (state.power === 'wide') {
+400|        inputState.paddle.width = 118;
+401|      } else if (state.power === 'slow') {
+402|        state.balls.forEach(ball => {
+403|          const speed = Math.sqrt(ball.vx ** 2 + ball.vy ** 2);
+404|          if (speed > 0) {
+405|            ball.vx /= 0.7;
+406|            ball.vy /= 0.7;
+407|          }
+408|        });
+409|      }
+410|      state.power = null;
+411|    }
+412|  }
+413|  
+414|  // Update drops
+415|  const newDrops = [];
+416|  state.drops.forEach(drop => {
+417|    drop.y += drop.vy * PHYSICS_STEP;
+418|    
+419|    if (drop.y < GAME_HEIGHT) {
+420|      // Check paddle collision
+421|      if (drop.y + 8 > paddle.y - 15 && drop.y - 8 < paddle.y &&
+422|          drop.x > paddle.x && drop.x < paddle.x + paddle.width) {
+423|        applyPowerUp(drop.type);
+424|        return;
+425|      }
+426|      newDrops.push(drop);
+427|    }
+428|  });
+429|  state.drops = newDrops;
+430|  
+431|  // Simulate ball physics (simplified)
+432|  state.balls.forEach((ball, idx) => {
+433|    if (ball.stuck || ball.lost) return;
+434|    
+435|    // Move ball
+436|    ball.x += ball.vx * PHYSICS_STEP;
+437|    ball.y += ball.vy * PHYSICS_STEP;
+438|    
+439|    // Wall bounces
+440|    if (ball.x - BALL_RADIUS < 0) {
+441|      ball.x = BALL_RADIUS;
+442|      ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx);
+443|    }
+444|    if (ball.x + BALL_RADIUS > GAME_WIDTH) {
+445|      ball.x = GAME_WIDTH - BALL_RADIUS;
+446|      ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx);
+447|    }
+448|    if (ball.y - BALL_RADIUS < 0) {
+449|      ball.y = BALL_RADIUS;
+450|      ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy);
+451|    }
+452|    
+453|    // Check if lost
+454|    if (ball.y > GAME_HEIGHT + 50) {
+455|      ball.lost = true;
+456|      state.lives--;
+457|      
+458|      if (state.lives > 0) {
+459|        gameState.gamePhase = 'life-lost';
+460|        showStatus(`Ball lost! ${state.lives} lives remaining`, 'error');
+461|        // Reset after 2 seconds
+462|        setTimeout(() => {
+463|          resetBall();
+464|          gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
+465|        }, 2000);
+466|      } else {
+467|        gameState.gamePhase = 'game-over';
+468|        finishRun('game-over');
+469|      }
+470|    }
+471|    
+472|    // Paddle collision
+473|    const paddleRect = {
+474|      x: paddle.x,
+475|      y: paddle.y - 15,
+476|      w: paddle.width,
+477|      h: 15
+478|    };
+479|    
+480|    if (circleRectCollision(ball, paddleRect)) {
+481|      ball.y = paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5;
+482|      ball.vy = -Math.abs(ball.vy);
+483|      state.combo = 1;
+484|    }
+485|    
+486|    // Brick collision
+487|    for (let i = 0; i < state.bricks.length; i++) {
+488|      const brick = state.bricks[i];
+489|      if (brick.destroyed) continue;
+490|      
+491|      const brickRect = { x: brick.x, y: brick.y, w: brick.w, h: brick.h };
+492|      
+493|      if (circleRectCollision(ball, brickRect)) {
+494|        ball.vy = -ball.vy;
+495|        
+496|        if (brick.type !== 'solid') {
+497|          if (brick.type === 'normal') {
+498|            brick.destroyed = true;
+499|            state.score += 100 * state.combo;
+500|            if (brick.drop) {
+501|              state.drops.push({
+502|                type: brick.drop,
+503|                x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,
+504|                y: brick.y + brick.h / 2,
+505|                vy: 100
+506|              });
+507|            }
+508|            state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);
+509|          } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
+510|            if (!brick.damaged) {
+511|              brick.damaged = true;
+512|              state.score += 75 * state.combo;
+513|              state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);
+514|            } else {
+515|              brick.destroyed = true;
+516|              state.score += 250 * state.combo;
+517|              if (brick.drop) {
+518|                state.drops.push({
+519|                  type: brick.drop,
+520|                  x: brick.x + brick.w / 2,
+521|                  y: brick.y + brick.h / 2,
+522|                  vy: 100
+523|                });
+524|              }
+525|              state.combo = Math.min(state.combo + 1, 5);
+526|            }
+527|          }
+528|        }
+529|      }
+530|    }
+531|  });
+532|  
+533|  // Check level completion
+534|  const breakableDestroyed = state.bricks.every(b => b.type === 'solid' || 
b.destroyed);
+535|  if (breakableDestroyed && state.balls.some(b => !b.lost)) {
+536|    state.score += 1000 * gameState.currentLevel;
+537|    gameState.gamePhase = 'level-complete';
+538|    
+539|    if (gameState.currentLevel === 10) {
+540|      gameState.gamePhase = 'completed';
+541|      finishRun('completed');
+542|    } else {
+543|      showStatus(`Level ${gameState.currentLevel} completed!`, 'success');
+544|      setTimeout(() => {
+545|        gameState.currentLevel++;
+546|        initializeGameState();
+547|        gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
+548|      }, 2000);
+549|    }
+550|  }
+551|  
+552|  updateHUD();
+553|}
+554|
+555|function circleRectCollision(circle, rect) {
+556|  const closestX = Math.max(rect.x, Math.min(circle.x, rect.x + rect.w));
+557|  const closestY = Math.max(rect.y, Math.min(circle.y, rect.y + rect.h));
+558|  
+559|  const dx = circle.x - closestX;
+560|  const dy = circle.y - closestY;
+561|  
+562|  return (dx * dx + dy * dy) < (BALL_RADIUS * BALL_RADIUS);
+563|}
+564|
+565|function resetBall() {
+566|  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
+567|  
+568|  const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
+569|  const paddle = inputState.paddle;
+570|  
+571|  state.balls = [{
+572|    x: paddle.x + paddle.width / 2,
+573|    y: paddle.y - BALL_RADIUS - 5,
+574|    vx: 0,
+575|    vy: 0,
+576|    stuck: true,
+577|    lost: false
+578|  }];
+579|  
+580|  showStatus('Press Space to launch', 'info');
+581|}
+582|
+583|async function saveGameState() {
+584|  if (!gameState.currentRun || gameState.gamePhase === 'menu') return;
+585|  
+586|  try {
+587|    await apiFetch('/api/game/save', {
+588|      method: 'POST',
+589|      body: JSON.stringify({
+590|        state: gameState.currentRun.state,
+591|        revision: gameState.lastRevision,
+592|        operationId: generateOperationId()
+593|      })
+594|    });
+595|  } catch (err) {
+596|    console.error('Failed to save:', err);
+597|  }
+598|}
+599|
+600|async function finishRun(outcome) {
+601|  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
+602|  
+603|  try {
+604|    const response = await apiFetch('/api/game/finish', {
+605|      method: 'POST',
+606|      body: JSON.stringify({
+607|        outcome,
+608|        level: gameState.currentLevel,
+609|        score: gameState.currentRun.state.score,
+610|        snapshot: gameState.currentRun.state,
+611|        revision: gameState.lastRevision,
+612|        operationId: generateOperationId()
+613|      })
+614|    });
+615|    
+616|    if (response.success) {
+617|      showStatus(`Run ${outcome}! Score: 
${gameState.currentRun.state.score}`, 'success');
+618|      gameState.currentRun = null;
+619|      gameState.lastRevision = 0;
+620|      loadLeaderboard();
+621|      loadRunHistory();
+622|      gameState.gamePhase = outcome === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 
'game-over';
+623|    }
+624|  } catch (err) {
+625|    console.error('Failed to finish run:', err);
+626|  }

+629|// ============ RENDERING ============
+630|
+631|function render() {
+632|  const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
+633|  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
+634|  
+635|  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0e27';
+636|  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, GAME_WIDTH, GAME_HEIGHT);
+637|  
+638|  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
+639|  
+640|  const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
+641|  
+642|  // Draw bricks
+643|  state.bricks.forEach(brick => {
+644|    if (brick.destroyed) return;
+645|    
+646|    if (brick.type === 'normal') {
+647|      ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';
+648|    } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
+649|      ctx.fillStyle = brick.damaged ? '#7ee787' : '#ff6b6b';
+650|    } else {
+651|      ctx.fillStyle = '#4a5568';
+652|    }
+653|    
+654|    ctx.fillRect(brick.x, brick.y, brick.w, brick.h);
+655|    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)';
+656|    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
+657|    ctx.strokeRect(brick.x, brick.y, brick.w, brick.h);
+658|  });
+659|  
+660|  // Draw paddle
+661|  const paddle = inputState.paddle;
+662|  ctx.fillStyle = '#56c7ff';
+663|  ctx.fillRect(paddle.x, paddle.y - 15, paddle.width, 15);
+664|  
+665|  // Draw balls
+666|  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
+667|  state.balls.forEach(ball => {
+668|    if (ball.lost) return;
+669|    ctx.beginPath();
+670|    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+671|    ctx.fill();
+672|  });
+673|  
+674|  // Draw drops
+675|  state.drops.forEach(drop => {
+676|    const colors = {
+677|      wide: '#ff9999',
+678|      slow: '#99ff99',
+679|      multiball: '#ffff99',
+680|      sticky: '#ff99ff'
+681|    };
+682|    ctx.fillStyle = colors[drop.type] || '#ffffff';
+683|    ctx.beginPath();
+684|    ctx.arc(drop.x, drop.y, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+685|    ctx.fill();
+686|  });

gameState.currentLevel || 1;

+700|

+703|    document.getElementById('power-name').textContent = state.power;
+704|    document.getElementById('power-timer').textContent = 
`${Math.ceil(state.powerSeconds)}s`;
+705|  } else {
+706|    document.getElementById('power-display').style.display = 'none';
+707|  }

+716|// ============ INPUT HANDLING ============
+717|
+718|function handleKeyDown(e) {
+719|  if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a') {
+720|    inputState.left = true;
+721|    if (gameState.currentRun && inputState.paddle) {
+722|      document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
+723|    }
+724|  }
+725|  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') {
+726|    inputState.right = true;
+727|    if (gameState.currentRun && inputState.paddle) {
+728|      document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
+729|    }
+730|  }
+731|  if (e.key === ' ') {
+732|    e.preventDefault();
+733|    launchBall();
+734|  }
+735|  if (e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P') {
+736|    e.preventDefault();
+737|    togglePause();
+738|  }
+739|  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+740|    e.preventDefault();
+741|    togglePause();
+742|  }
+743|  if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {
+744|    e.preventDefault();
+745|    if (gameState.gamePhase === 'game-over' || gameState.gamePhase === 
'completed') {
+746|      restartRun();
+747|    }
+748|  }
+749|}
+750|
+751|function handleKeyUp(e) {
+752|  if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a') inputState.left = false;
+753|  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd') inputState.right = false;
+754|}
+755|
+756|function handleCanvasMouseMove(e) {
+757|  const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
+758|  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
+759|  const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
+760|  
+761|  if (gameState.currentRun) {
+762|    inputState.paddle.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x - inputState.paddle.width 
/ 2, GAME_WIDTH - inputState.paddle.width));
+763|    document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
+764|  }
+765|}
+766|
+767|function handleCanvasTouchMove(e) {
+768|  const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
+769|  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
+770|  const touch = e.touches[0];
+771|  const x = touch.clientX - rect.left;
+772|  
+773|  if (gameState.currentRun) {
+774|    inputState.paddle.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x - inputState.paddle.width 
/ 2, GAME_WIDTH - inputState.paddle.width));
+775|    document.getElementById('assist-paddle-toggle').checked = false;
+776|  }

+778|
+779|function launchBall() {
+780|  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
+781|  
+782|  const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
+783|  const stuck = state.balls.find(b => b.stuck);
+784|  
+785|  if (stuck) {
+786|    stuck.stuck = false;
+787|    stuck.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 400;
+788|    stuck.vy = -300;
+789|    gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
+790|    if (!animationFrameId) startGameLoop();
+791|  }
+792|}
+793|
+794|function togglePause() {
+795|  if (gameState.gamePhase === 'playing') {
+796|    gameState.gamePhase = 'paused';
+797|    showStatus('Paused', 'info');
+798|  } else if (gameState.gamePhase === 'paused') {
+799|    gameState.gamePhase = 'playing';
+800|    if (!animationFrameId) startGameLoop();
+801|  }
+802|}
+803|
+804|function restartRun() {
+805|  if (gameState.currentLevel) {
+806|    gameState.currentRun = null;
+807|    startNewRun(gameState.currentLevel);
+808|  }
+809|}
+810|
+811|function applyPowerUp(type) {
+812|  if (!gameState.currentRun) return;
+813|  
+814|  const state = gameState.currentRun.state;
+815|  
+816|  if (type === 'wide') {
+817|    inputState.paddle.width = Math.min(177, inputState.paddle.width * 1.5);
+818|  } else if (type === 'slow') {
+819|    state.balls.forEach(ball => {
+820|      if (!ball.stuck) {
+821|        ball.vx *= 0.7;
+822|        ball.vy *= 0.7;
+823|      }
+824|    });
+825|  } else if (type === 'multiball') {
+826|    const moving = state.balls.find(b => !b.stuck && !b.lost);
+827|    if (moving && state.balls.length < 2) {
+828|      state.balls.push({
+829|        x: moving.x - 20,
+830|        y: moving.y - 20,
+831|        vx: moving.vx * 0.8,
+832|        vy: moving.vy * 0.8,
+833|        stuck: false,
+834|        lost: false
+835|      });
+836|    }
+837|  } else if (type === 'sticky') {
+838|    const moving = state.balls.find(b => !b.stuck && !b.lost);
+839|    if (moving) {
+840|      moving.stuck = true;
+841|    }
+842|  }
+843|  
+844|  if (state.power === type) {
+845|    state.powerSeconds = 20;
+846|  } else {
+847|    state.power = type;
+848|    state.powerSeconds = 20;
+849|  }
+850|  
+851|  showStatus(`${type} collected!`, 'success');
+852|}

+871|        <div>Score: ${entry.score} | Level ${entry.level}</div>
+872|      `;

+951|function switchTab(e) {
+952|  const tabName = e.target.getAttribute('data-tab');
+953|  
+954|  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
+955|    btn.classList.remove('active');
+956|  });
+957|  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(content => {
+958|    content.classList.remove('active');
+959|  });
+960|  
+961|  e.target.classList.add('active');
+962|  
document.getElementById(`tab-content-${tabName}`).classList.add('active');
